2b1(rgyud): pa'i ting nge 'dzin brgya stong phrag mang po thob par bgyi lagaso//bcom ldan 'das 'di ltar yang sa phyogs gang du snying po 'di spyod pa'i sa phyogs de dang/dbang phyug
2b2(rgyud): dang /dbang phyug chen po la sogs pa lha'i bu khri nyis stong bsrung ba dang/sba ba'i slad du gnas par rig par bgyi lags so//bcom ldan 'das gang na don yod pa phun
2b3(rgyud): sum 'tshogs pa'i snying po 'di spyod pa'i phyogs de mchod rten du bkur bar 'gyur lags so//bcom ldan 'dis sems can de dag ni sangs rgyas bye ba khrag
2b4(rgyud): khrig stong phrag brgya la dge ba'i rtsa ba bskyed par 'gyur lag+saso//gang zhig don yod pa'i zhags pa'i snying po 'di nyan par bgyid na/bcom ldan 'das
2b5(rgyud): sdig pa spyod pa 'am/sdig pa'i chos kun du spyod pa 'am/'phags pa la bskur pa 'debs pa 'am/dam pa'i chos spong ba 'am/mnar med par gzhal ba 'am/sangs

3a4(rgyud): 'brum bu phra mo 'am/lhog pa 'am;mtshan ma brdol pa 'am/'brum bu 'am;rjed byed dam/byad sdems saM;gzhan gyis bsod cing/mching ba 'am brdeg

3b2(rgyud): par bgyid cing te/skur pa ma mchis pa dang/gzugs ma mchis pa dang/rnam par mi rtog pa dang/kun du 'byung ba ma mchis pa dang/ring du 'gro ba ma mchis pa dang/bgyid
3b3(rgyud): pa ma mchis pa dang/gos pa ma mchis pa dang/sems mnyam pa nyid dang bral ba dang/phung po lnga dang bral ba ste/rnal 'byor 'dis sangs rgyas rjes su dran

4a1(rgyud): @#//smad de|rdo la btags nas/bdag nyid la byugs nas/tsan dan nam ga pur ram/bla rtsi de 'di snyam du bdag la 'khros sam/yongs su smrad

4b4(rgyud): chab kyis mi 'tshal /rgyal po'i mthus 'phrogs pa'i rngo mi thog par 'gyuro//de'i las kyi mthu' rnams rgyas par 'gyur te/ser bas ma 
4b5(rgyud): lagaso//chab kyi 'jigs pa dang/rlung dang me'i 'jigs pa Ima lags so//don yod pa'i zhags pa'i snying po thal ba dang chu la lan bdun bzlas

4b8(rgyud): la 'jigs pa byung na myur du zhi bar 'gyuro//de la mi ma lag+sa pa'i 'jigs pa 'byung bar ma 'gyur te/byad kyi 'jigs pa ma lags/mkha' 'gro ma yis 'jigs 
5a1(rgyud): @#//pa ma lags te/de la nyid mngos pa dang/nye ba'i nyon mongs pa drag po 'byung bar mi 'gyuro//me dang mtshon gyis 'gum par mi 'gyuro//de la yang lha rnaMs kyis rtag tu
5a2(rgyud): rgyun mi chad par bsrung ba dang skyob pa dang/sbed pa'i phyir gnas par mi 'gyuro//gang dang gang du skye bar 'gyur bar byaMs pa dang/snying rje dang dga' ba dang/btang snyoms dang bral bar
5a3(rgyud): mi 'gyur te/de phan yon nyi shu rtsa gcig po 'di dag thob par 'gyur lagaso//gzhan yang chos brgyad thob par 'gyur te/brgyad gang zhe na/'gum pa'i dus su 'phags pa

5a7(rgyud): mi 'gyuro;nyin gcig bzhin du bzlas brjod bgyi'o//chang dang sha dang;sku dong dang;keng du dang/sgog skya dang;'dres pa dang/lhag rol dang/khyad par du don gnyer ba spang bar bya'o//

5b2(rgyud): mi bgyi'o//'di ltar dri ma dang bral zhing 'phra dog dang srer sna sbangs pa ni byang chub seMs rba rnaMs lags te/sems kyi don pa'i slad du/sangs rgyas byang chub thob par 'gyur bas/byang chub seMs

7a3(rgyud): la btags par bgyi'o//so na pa la ni shing ka ra ba ri'i so shing gis so//'tshams bcad pa ni mtshon sna lnga'i skud pa la lan nyi shu rtsa gcig bzlas brjod bgyis te seng ldeng gyi phur pa bzhi la bcings n+sa/phyogs bzhir

7a6(rgyud): pa dang gcod pa dang 'gyed pa dang/skur pa 'debs pa thams cad la ni chab la bzlas brjod bgyis te la bshal par bar bgyi'o//dpung dang yul dang rgyal srid yul rnams bsrung ba la ni bum pa gang gzhag la 
7a7(rgyud): la rtsang ma 'am/gos rtsang ma dgos pas mchod pa rgya chen por bgyis nas zhi ba chen por 'gyur ro//des yang chab kyis gtor par bgyi ste/sems can thaMs cad bsrung ba bgyis par 'gyur ro//yam kyi 
7a8(rgyud): nad dang gnod pa dang 'tshe ba thams cad zhi bar bgyi pa dang/blags pa lta ba rnams ni tsan dan la lan nyi shu rtsa gcig bzlas brjod bgyi ste/snying khar byug par bgyi'o//'tshams ma mchis pa'i las thams 
7b1(rgyud): cad pas bgyi ba la ni rtag tu bzlas brjod bgyiso//khyim bsrung ba la ni pad ma'i sbyin sreg bgyiso//sems can thams cad skyang ba dang 'byung po'i 'jig+sa pa dang/gdon thams cad las bsrung ba la ni tsan dan gyi sbyin
7b2(rgyud): sreg bgyiso//dza ya dang/pi dza ya dang na ku li dang/g+han d+ha ku li dang/ba ru ni dang/tse 'dra ba du ni dang pa ri ni dang/a p+ha ya pa ni dang/i ni pra pa ni dang/g+han d+ha kri ya ku dang/rgya spes dang tsi kra dang/mahA tsa kra dang/bi sU tran
7b3(rgyud): ta dang/so mar dza dang/su nan da rnaMs la lan brgya rtsa brgyad sbyin sreg bgyis te nor bur bgyis nas spyi bo'am dpung pa la btags pa bsrel bar bgyi'o//khye'u rnams ni mgul par bgyi'o bu mo rnaMs la ni sta zur n+sa te/dpal
7b4(rgyud): dang ldan par bgyid pa dang bkra mi shis pa rab tu/zhi bar bgyid pa'i nor bu 'di bsrel na thams cad las bsrung pa bgyis par 'gyuro//dug dang sme mi tshig lags so//dug gi gnod pa bskyed par mi
7b5(rgyud): 'gyuro/skye na yang gnod pa skyes par mi bgyid cing/myur du zhi bar 'gyuro//rlung dang ser ba gcod pa dang/char gyi rgyun dang ser ba zlog pa la ni/shing kar ba ra'i lcug ma ste las thaMs cad bgyid pa/'phags pa
7b6(rgyud): spyan ras gzig+sa kyi dbang phyug gi snying po grub pa dam pa ma bsgrubs par las 'di rnams bgyid par 'gyur lagaso//de nas bsgrubs par 'tshal na/chog ni ras la lhag ma ma yin pa'i tshon rtsis sangs rgyas dang 'phags
7b7(rgyud): pa spyan ras gzigs dbang phyug gi gzugs brnyan ras pa'i thor tshug+sa dang/e na ya'i lpags pa dang/phyugs bdag gi cha lugs 'chang ba/brgyan thaMs cad kyis brgyan pa bri bar bya ste/ri mo mkhan gso sbyong can

8a1(rgyud): @#//drug bcu rtsa bzhi 'am/gzhan yang lha bshos sha dang khrag dang ma 'dres pa bshams shing 'dres la/a ka ru'i gdug pas gdugs te/lan stong rtsa brgyad bzlas brjod bgyi'o//nyin zhag gcig gam/nyin
8a2(rgyud): zhag gsum du snyung bar bgyis na/zas dkar gsum du tshal te dus gsum du khrus bgyis nas/gos rtsang ma dgos nas/'phags pa spyan ras gzigs dbang phyug bdag nyid mchis nas/bsam pa thaMs cad yongasu rdzogs
8a3(rgyud): par bgyid;;ldong ros sam/mig sman la bzlas brjod bgyis te;mig la bskus na de mi snang bar 'gyuro//namakha' la 'chi bar 'gyur zhing don yod pa'i ye shes bkod pa zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin thob par 'gyuro//ci 'tshal

8a7(rgyud): zer sna tshogs 'gyed pa'i lus bcom ldan 'das gang na de logasu thal mo sbyar ba btud de zhal gyi dkyil 'khor rgyas shing rdzogs la zhi ba 'dzum pa'i gzhin gyis/byams pa dang snying rje chen po'i mthu yid la byed pas rig+sa bzhi'i 'jig rten
8a8(rgyud): la phan pa dang/snying rje bas don thaMs cad grub pa'i rigs sngags 'chang ba rnams kyis bla na med pa de kho na nyid grub pa mchog sbyin pa thob par bya ba'i phyir/bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsol to//bcom ldan 'das bdag la
8b1(rgyud): don yod pa'i rgyal po'i cho ga zhib mo 'di mchis te/'di pad ma chen po'i rgyal po'i dkyil 'khor de kho na nyid chen pos 'phags pa'i snying por gyur ba de kha na nyid grub pa de dkyil 'khor 'di'i phyag rgya dang/gsang sngags dang snying
8b2(rgyud): po rim par phye pa gsang ba dam pa rnam par grol ba'i dkyil 'khor dam tshig dam bsgrub pa gsang ba 'di bcom ldan 'das kyi spyan sngar sgrag par bgyi'o//lha dang klu dang gnod sbyin dang/dri za dang lha ma yin dang/namakha' lding dang/

8b6(rgyud): du ma'i phreng ba dang/spos kyi glang po che'i sna lta bu'i phyag rgya ste/byang chub sems pa seMs dpa' chen po 'phags pa spyan ras gzigs dbang phyug gi spyi bor gzhag nas klang pa la byugs te/'di skad ces bka' stsal to//sems
8b7(rgyud): can dag pa dam pa snying rje chen po dang ldan pa khyod smros shig/de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyis byin gyis rlabs pa gang ga'i klung byed ma 'brum phrag brgya stong gi bye ma'i rdul phra rab snyed kyi de bzhin gshegs pa rnams gyis kyang
8b8(rgyud): byin gyis rlabs shing/rjesu yid rang bar bgyi'o//de nas byang chub sems pa sems dpa' chen po 'phags ba spyan ras gzigs dbang phyag gis phyogs bcur bltas te/bcom ldan 'das kyi spyan sngar rang gi de
9a1(rgyud): @#//kho na nyid gsang ba'i sngags 'di dag gsol to//A+o~M pad ma a sta ma hA a mo g+ha pa shA sa rta sa ma ya hri ta yan/gis phyogs bcur bltas te/bcom ldan 'das kyi spyan sngar rang gi tsa ra tsa ra

9a3(rgyud): 'di brag dang /nags tshal dang rtsi shing du bcas dang /ri rab khor yug dang khor yug chen po dang bcas pa rnams pa drug tu g.yos shing lha'i gnas rab tu ldeg par gyur to//thams cad 'dar 'dar por gyur to//

9a7(rgyud): klags pa'i cho ga de nyid bsgrub pa rgyas pa dang bcas pa smros shig/spyan ras gzigs dbang phyug gis gsol pa/bcom ldan 'das gsang ba'i snying po'i gzungs 'di lan cig brjod pa tsam gyis/

10a1(rgyud): @#//zhag+sa pa'i snying po'i lag na thogs pa'i zhag+sa pa 'bar bar 'gyur ro//rigs sngags 'chang bar de snying dga' bar dang/spyi bo'i rtsug 'bar bar 'gyur ro//'di ni

10b8(rgyud): bcu'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing rnams su 'dug pa'i srin po 'khris pas rgyas par 'gyur ro//srin 'khrigs pa de dag gis sangs rgyas dang byang chub sems pa thaMs cad dang/lha dang klu dang

11b6(rgyud): mang du byas pa dang/khyod kyis bya ba gang yin pa byas pa dang/khyod don yod pa'i zhags pa'i snying po rnam par grol ba'i dkyil 'khor phyag rgya chen po dang/gsang sngags rnam par phye ba'i snying po bsgrub pa ni grub zin

11b8(rgyud): snying po la thug gi bar du sangs rgyas kyi zhing nas sangs rgyas kyi zhing du 'gro bar 'gyuro//zhes phyag g.yas pa spyi bor 'jog par 'gyur/'jig rten pa'i dngos grub thams cad brtsol cing 'jig rten las 'das pa'i
12a1(rgyud): @#//dngos grub grub ste/'di ni dam pa bsgrub pa yin no//zhes bdag nyid sad nas dga' ba mthong ste/sgrib pa thams cad dang bral zhing yongs su dga' bar 'gyur ro//'phags pa spyan

12a5(rgyud): bcing bar bya'o//de nas tshes bcu bzhi la thob mar dge sbyong dang bram ze rnaMs la ci 'byor ba'i yon dang bcas pa'i mchod ston bstsal ba bgyi'o//de nas bdag nyid longs spyad par bgyi'o//gang
12a6(rgyud): gi tshe yang sangs rgyas la lta ba dang /byang chub sems rbal lta ba dang /lha rnams la lta bar 'tshal na de'i tshe bdug pa'i rgyal po la dgug pa'i gsang sngags lan nyi shu rtsa gcig bzlas brjod bgyis te/sangs
12a7(rgyud): rgyas bcom ldan 'das dgug par bgyi'o//dgug pa tsam gyi mod la zhal ston par 'gyur zhing /dge ba 'am mi dge ba thaMs cad dang nyams pa 'am 'bri ba de dag thams cad gsung bar 'gyur ro//ci tsam tshe'i mtha'
12a8(rgyud): cha thug gi bar du gsung pa 'am/'gro ba'i 'ong ba gsung bar 'gyur ro//de nas gsang sngags kyi cho ga dang /bsgrub pa'i don dang las kyi cho ga yang ji lta ba bzhin bshad par bgyi'o//A+o ma d+ha rang 
12b1(rgyud): a mo g+ha dz+wa ya de/tsu ru tsu ru swAhA/don yod pa'i rgyal po'i snying po'i gsang sngagaso//A+o~M pad ma pA sha d+ha ra a mo g+ha pa rang san tso da ya hUM/de bzhin gshegs pa dang byang chub sems pa thams cad

12b4(rgyud): yA du b+hu pa ni su ru su ru bi ma li hUM/dgug pa'i gsang sngags te/gu gul gyi dud pa lan brgya rtsa brgyad bzlas brjod bgyis na/chu dang me tog sres pas phyogs bcur gtor bar bgyis

12b6(rgyud): sems dpa' thams cad dang nyan thos dang rang sangs rgyas thams cad dang /dkyil 'khor thams cad na gnas pa'i lha tshogs la sogs pa spyan drang ba'i gsang sngags te ral ba spyi lugs dang /chab dang me 
12b7(rgyud): tog dang tshems shing la bdug pa'i rgyal pos bdugs te/thams cad la lan brgya rtsa brgyad bzlasabarjod bgyis te/thams cad spyan drang ba'i rig+sa sngagaso//A+o~M pad ma a mo g+ha badzra a de ShA na ku ru ku ru
12b8(rgyud): swAhA//sa'i dkyil 'khor bcing ba ste/chab dang yungs dkar la lan brgya rtsa brgyad bzas brjod bgyis nas sa la rtor na dpag tshad sum rgyas kun nas 'khor ba'i sa'i dkyil 'khor rdo rjer byin gyi rlabs
13a1(rgyud): @#//par 'gyuro//de thams cad bsrungs bas ni rdo rje sra ba'i semasu 'gyur ro//dgra rnams kyis mi mthong ba dang/bgegs dang log 'dren dang srin po dang/gnod sbyin dang sha za dang/'byung po dang/
13a2(rgyud): rjed dang ro langs dang/'dod dgur sbyar ba'i gzugs thams cad kyis mi mthong ste/rnam pa thams cad du mi mthong bas rdo rje sra bar 'gyur ro//A+oM a mo g+ha sad sha mo du ra si ha sa na du Ti/si ri si ri hU ma
13a3(rgyud): gdan gyi gsang sngags sngags te/yungs dkar la stong rtsa brgyad bzlas brjod bgyis te/sa la rtor na bad ma chen po'i gzhal med khang gi gdan du byin gyis rlabs nas legs par phul bar gyur ro//de bzhin
13a4(rgyud): gshegs pa thams cad la rin po che sna bdun las byas pa'i seng ge'i ba dan du byin gyis rlabs na/legs par phul bar bgyi'o/rigs sngags 'chang gis stan la chab dang yungs dkar

18a8(rgyud): pad me dane hU~M/ril ba mgrin rings kyi gsang sngags te/lan 'dun bzlas so//A+o rad na di bye;rad nu gu b+ha ba ti swAhA//ga ra saM b+hu ra ni swAhA/bum pa'i gsang sngags te/lan gdun bzlas//A+oM bi tsid 
18b1(rgyud): tri the la na/nA rang ga bi b+ha ga ta yA/sang sho d+h+ya tu tra swAhA//mtshon rtsi'i gsang sngags te/lan bdun bzlaso//A+o~M bdi r+ya a ba lo ga ni/bi b+ha dza ya du pa man de na pa ma ra na swAhA//lan nyi shu rtsi gcig bzlas 
18b2(rgyud): brjod bgyis te/so so rab kyi bar bgyi'o//A+o~M na na pra ha ra na a mo g+ha mud tra/dz+wa la dz+wa la swAhA/phyag mtshan bri ba ste/lan bdun bzlaso//o~M ma a mo g+ha rang na su tra d+ha re ki na ki ni swAhA//thig skud kyi gsang
18b3(rgyud): sngags te/lan brgya rtsa brgyad bzlas brjod dkyil 'khor ri mo bri ba'i sngags sam/gzhan ci yang rung ba la btab par bya'o//A+o~M pi bu la pi re pra pe sha ya/a mo g+ha pad me hUM/sgo'i phya dbye ba ste/sgo 
18b4(rgyud): re rer yang lan nyi shu rtsa gcig bzlaso//A+o~M si b+ha ga te su ma ga la/dwa ra b+ha ba na ti/A+o~M dbyi b+h+ya dza na/a mo g+ha pan shan pan pa re gi ri gi ri hU~M/A+o~M sa b+hu rna ru bya dbyi b+hi dz+wa ni/A+o mo g+ha pad 
18b5(rgyud): me swAhA//lan bdun bzlas pa bgyi'o//A+o~M pi bi d+ha du ba ha ra pa ga ra na/tsa la tsa la a mo g+ha ti swAhA//lan nyi shu rtsa gcig bzlas so/A+o~M dib bya ga na d+ha nu pa ri ni/a mo g+ha b+ha le d+ha ra swAhA//
18b6(rgyud): mchod yon gyi gsang sngags te/lan bdun bzlaso//A+o~M g+ha na d+ha sbar ni sa man d+ha/oM mo g+ha pa spur hU~M/dri'i sngagasang sngags te/lan bdunabazlaso//A+o~M ni ra ma la ka ya sho d+ha ni/dib pa ga na d+ha pra pu ra 
18b7(rgyud): ni pu ru ya hU~M/byug pa'i gsang sngags te/lan bdud bzlas so//A+o~M dib bya g+han d+ha pra pu ra ni sa ga ra dza la dib bya/a di Sh+Ta de ka la sho d+ha ri pu rna pu ra yu dza sa d+hi swAhA//bum pa'i dri chab kyi gsang sngags
18b8(rgyud): te/lan brgya rtsa brgyad bzlaso//o~M sa ra sa ra sa gra pa pa rbad ti bya d+ha Sh+Ta de saM pu ra ya hU~M/rtor ma ro sna tshogs can gyi gsang sngags te/lam nyi shu rtsa gcig bzlaso//A+o~M dib bya b+ho d+ha na saM bu ra ya//
19a1(rgyud): @#//b+hi su la pra pe swAhA/kha zas kyi gsang sngags te/lan nyi shu rtsa gcig bzlas so//A+o~M dri shug kla b+ho dza na pa li pi pu la ra sa pra pe ste su ra pra su ra hUM/zas dkar gsum gyi rtor ma 'am/gzhan la yang 
19a2(rgyud): lan nyi shu rtsa gcig bzlaso//A+o~M pa la ba ti swa ra ba ti dibi bya ba swa ti/ta ra ta ra du swAhA/'bras bu'i gsang sngags te/lan bdun bzlaso//A+o~M dib bya ku su mo dpal nan pus Sh+pa pra pa ri Sha ni b+ha ba swAhA//me tog 
19a3(rgyud): gi sngags te/lan bdun brlaso//o~M b+hi ma la d+ha la ta pu Sh+pa kun Sh+ri ta pa nas swa ti/a ha ra sa~M pu rna gu~M b+he swAhA//me tog rtor ba'i gsang sngags te/lan ba dun bzlaso//A+o~M mahA d+hu pa me g+ha pa 
19a4(rgyud): la san tsA da ya sa man te hU~M swAhA/dgug pa'i gsang sngags te/lan nyi  shu rtsa gcig bzlaso/A+o~M a tha ra pa ni/dz+wa la dz+wa la de mu khye swAhA/me'i gsang sngags te/lan dun bzlas so//
19a5(rgyud): A+o~M pa khyi d+ha ma ni ka na ka rad na tha la b+hu Sha ni/ma di ma Ni a mo g+hA ma ni swAhA//cha zhing brgyan pa'i sraM gsang sngags te/lan bdud bzlaso//A+o~M pad mo ka ra bi ma la na kha a gu li sa ma sa ma/sarba ta thA ga ta 
19a6(rgyud): a d+hi ShI ti swAhA/thal mo sbyar ba'i rigs sngags te/lan nyi shu rtsa gcig bzlas so//A+o~M sarba ku no than ta shi ro ba na ya na ma ska ro mi//mi li mi li du ri ni swAhA//phyag bgyi pa'i gsang sngags te/lanabadun 
19a7(rgyud): bzlas so//A+o~M sha ra di sha a b+hi b+han d+ha na pa ra du d+hu~M/skor ba'i gsang sngags te/lan nya shu rtsa gcig bzlaso//A+o~M b+hi d+ha yud d+ha dza li ta pa ni pra s+phu ra hU~M/thal mo'i sngags te/A+o~M dib bya a mo g+ha pa pu 
19a8(rgyud): la s+thu re d+ha ra ni ra g+ha shi ru ti Na/ra na ra Na hU~M/dril bu'i gsang sngags te/lan nyi shu tsa gcig bzlas so//yang na brgya rtsa brgyad do//A+o~M ra sh+ma dz+wa la sa man te na a ba b+ha sa ma ya mahA ma ni d+ha na shi khya swAhA/
19b1(rgyud): mar me'i gsang sngags te/lan brgya rtsa brgyad bzlaso//A+o d+hu ru d+hu ru a ba ha ya shi g+ha dzra be hUM/dgug pa'i gsang sngags te/gu gul gyi bdug pa la lan brgya rtsa brgyad bzlaso //A+o tu Sh+Tha sad dA bi bo d+ha Ni sa ma 
19b2(rgyud): sa ma ya bi ri swAhA//dam tshig gi gsang sngags te/lan nyi shu rtsa gcig bzlas so//A+o bi tsa tri pa pu la pra par d+ha ni hUM/bgrang phreng gi gsang sngags te/lan brgyartsa brgyad bzlaso//A+o bi tsi hra ra/rad na dz+wa 
19b3(rgyud): la dib bya ku su ma ma ba re Sh+wa ra ti swAhA//me tog phreng ba'i gsang sngags te/lan dun bzlaso//oM dib bya la tsa ni a g+ha pra ra ris rba byid r+ya a d+hi Sh+Tha na sid de shu d+hi ba bi lo ki ti swAhA//dgug 
19b4(rgyud): pa'i gsang sngags te/lan nyi shi rtsa gsum bzlaso=/A+o~M a mo g+ha bar me sa man ta shu b+ha ni/ta ra ta ra gang dza saM b+ha naM swAhA//slar gshegs gsol pa'i gsang sngags te/lan nya shu rtsa 
19b5(rgyud): gcig bzlaso//oM dibi bya sa ma bur Ni N+ya ga bi lo ka ni hUM/kha ra sil gyi gsang sngags te/lan nye shu rtsa gcig bzlaso//A+o~M pad mo pra bu ra yang a mo g+ha ba ti pa man ta pra b+he swAhA//lhung gzed kyi gsang 
19b6(rgyud): sngags te/lan nyi shu rtsa gcig bzlaso///A+o~M tur b+ha s+tha ra b+hing d+ha a d+hi Sh+Tha ta pra pa ra su ru su ru ri shi su dzi ti swAhA/rtsa ding ba'i gsang sngags te//lan nyi shu rtsa gcig bzlos//A+o~M dza la du ma dza la du ma 
19b7(rgyud): dz+wa la shi khyir shi gar b+he swAhA//sbyin sreg gsang sngags te/lan brgya rtsa brgyad bzlaso//A+o~M pad mi ni pad ma sa ni ba rad pra ba re ni swAhA//'bab chu dang mtsho'i gsang sngags te/lan bdun bzlaso/
19b8(rgyud): A+o~M khad ga tsA ri ni ga ma ga ma ya pra dwa pra tsa ri ni hUM/len pa'i gsang sngags te lan brgya rtsa brgyad bzlaso//A+o~M sarba tra a mo b+ha b+hi shi ka tshud ta ba'i ma le a b+hi shi na tsa tu man pra ba ni b+hu dza pad ma tA dz+wa swAhA//
20a1(rgyud): @#//dbang skur ba'i gsang sngags te/lan nyi shu rtsa gcig bzlaso//rigasngags 'chang gis gsang g+sas 'di dag thams cad so sor cho ga zhib mo'i cho ga bsgrub pa ji lta ba bzhin du thams cad so sor
20a2(rgyud): sbyar nas cho bsgrubs na/dgos pa rgya chen po 'byor ba'i mthu chen po 'di dag dang/me tog dang bdug pa dang/dri dang phreng ba dang/byug pa dang/rin po che'i snod sna tshogs dang/nor bu sna tshogs kyi gzhal
20a3(rgyud): med khang chen po 'byung bar 'gyuro//lha'i me tog dang bdug pa dang /ba dan sna tshogs pa 'byung bar 'gyuro//ro brgya dang ldan pa'i kha zas kyi mchog dang/ro tshogs kyi rnam par 'byung
20a4(rgyud): bar 'gyuro//tsi tsam de bzhin gshegs pa la 'os pa'i gzhal med khang rnams 'byung bar 'gyuro//byang chub sems dpa'i gzhal med khang rnams ji lta ba'i de lta bu dag 'byung bar
20a5(rgyud): 'gyuro//klu rnams dang gnod sbyin rnams dang /lha ma yin rnams dang/namakha' lding rnams dang/mi 'am ci rnams dang/lto 'phye chen po rnaMs kyi so so gzhal med khang ji lta bu de dag 'byung bar 'gyur/
20a6(rgyud): mi rnams kyi khang pa ji lta ba dang/kha zas kyi mchog ji lta bu dag 'byung bar 'gyuro//de lta bas na dgos pa rnam pa thams cad dang/de kho na nyid bsgrub pa ji skad gsungs pa'i cho ga zhib mo dang/cho
20a7(rgyud): ga dang gsang sngags bsgrub pa rnams bgyi'o//de nas rigs sngags 'tshang gis bsgrub pa bla na med pa thams cad thob par 'gyuro//gal te rigs sngags 'tshang gi las thaMs cad yongasu rdzogs par
20a8(rgyud): bgyis te/don yod pa'i zhag+sa pa'i snying po 'di brjod pa tsam gyis rigasngags 'chang gi 'dun du bdag rang gi gzugs nye bar mchis nas/bsam pa dang re ba thams cad yongasu rdzogs par bgyi'o//sdig
20b1(rgyud): pa dang gnod pa thams cad 'jig par bgyi'o//yongasu dag pa'i lha chen po ltar lus dag par bgyi'o//cho ga zhib mo dang phyag rgya dang gsang sngags dang/snying po dang dkyil 'khor dang/don yod pa'i rgyal po'i
20b2(rgyud): rnam par dkyil 'khor grub pa'i mchog thaMs cad brtsal bar bgyi'o//rig sngags 'chang 'khor lo bsgyur ba chen po'i dbang skur bar bgyi'o//de kho na nyid yang dag par bsgrub pa 'grub par brtsal bar
20b3(rgyud): bgyi'o//don yod pa'i zhag+sa pa 'jig rten pa dang 'jig rten las 'das pa bsgrub pa'i grub po rtsal bar bgyi'o//shin du rgya che zhing bag tshal ba ma mchis pa dang/mchod pa dang bkur bsti chen po 'jig
20b4(rgyud): rten thaMs cad kyi mchog khyad par 'phag+sa shing nor 'du ba'i dkra shis dang dpal brtsal bar bgyi'o//'di la yid gnyis dang sau nyi skyed par mi bgyi zhing/dngos po gzhan du yid la mi bgyi zhing/
20b5(rgyud): sems can thaMs cad la byis pa dang/snying rje dang dga' ba dang/btang snyoms kyis brtse ba la mchog du brtson par bgyi'o//bu cig pa bzhin du sems can du gyur pa thaMs cad la mchog du brtse bar bgyi'o//bden
20b6(rgyud): par smra ba dang bden pa la dga' ba dang/snyan par smra ba dang snying rjes lhag pa'i bsam pas dmigs n+sa/don yod pa'i zhags pa'i snying po bzlas par bgyi'o//bzlas brjod kyi cho ga bgyi zhing phyag rgya dang gsang
20b7(rgyud): sngags kying bzlas par bgyi'o//phyogs bcu'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing thams cad du rtog+sa pa'i de bzhin gsheg+sa pa rnaMs yid la bgyis n+sa/sangs rgyas rjesu dran pa sgom par bgyi'o//sangs rgyas dang byang chub seMs pa thaMs
20b8(rgyud): la phyag 'tshalo//'phag+sa pa mog na po spyan ras gzig+sa dbang phyug la phyag 'gyis na mngon sum du bcas par bgyi'o//bcoMldad 'phag+sa pa spyan ras gzig+sa dbang phyag gi ri'i mo'i gzug+sa la phyag bgyi'o//mdun na rtsa'i
21a1(rgyud): @#//gding ba btang nas/dkyil mo dkrung bcas te mchis nas/gzhan mi smra bar bzlas pa bgyis te/'di ni gsang ba'i de kho na nyid bsgrub pa'o//de nas phyag rgya dang gsang sngags brjod par bgyi'o//
21a2(rgyud): bdag la don yod pa'i zhags pa'i snying po'i de kho na nyid grub par gyur cig/'jig rten dbang phyug mgon po thugs rje can/sems can la byas pa sems dpa' dag/lag na pad ma seMs can thaMs cad la byaMs pa'i
21a3(rgyud): bdag nyid can gyis byin gyis brlos kyis/phyogs bcu'i de bzhin gshegs pa kun dang/lha dang klu dang gnod sbyin dang/srid po lha min gsang ba pa/'jig rten skyong ba tshangs pa dang/drag pa khyab 
21a4(rgyud): 'jug gzhon nu sogs/gshin rje chu lha lus ngan dang/drang srong tshogs dang de bzhin du/zla nyid kun bgyid gzhon nu dang/de bzhin dbang phyug tshe dbang bdag phyag rgya bcings ba tsam gyis ni/
21a5(rgyud): de dag myur du grub par 'gyur//sgrol ma mdun ni 'du bar 'gyur//de bzhin pha rol phyin bcu dang/mgrin sngon de bzhin gos dkar can/de bzhin pad ma gar gyi dbang rgyal dang rnam rgyal nyid
21a6(rgyud): dang ni lcag sgrog rdo rje lcags dang//lha mo 'khro gnyer ma ma kyi//dpal mo pho nyi mo dang ni/'jig med dpal dang byangs can dang//pad ma kun 'di ud pa la//pad ma'i so can de bzhin du/ti se'i ni rtse ma na/
21a7(rgyud): 'phrog ma 'byung po'i ma dge dang/de bzhin la ga na rdo rje bcu//mthu chen ci bzhin rigs 'dzin debang/rdo rje 'chang chen de bzhin du/bcu gsum lha dbang lhan cig dang/sum bcu gnyis gnas lha dag na/gsang
21a8(rgyud): sngags phyag rgyas bcings tsam gyis//de dag thaMs cad 'du bar 'gyur//phyogs bcu'i de bzhin gshegs pa thaMs cad kyis rnam par gzigs shing sngon par mdzad do//de'i med la byang chub seMs pa rnaMs kyi
21b1(rgyud): rna par gzigs shing rtag tu mchog brtsol bar 'gyuro//rtag tu mog na po spyan ras gzig+sa dbang phyug phyag stong spyan stong pa/phyag sna tshogs pa 'dzin pa lta 'tshal bar bgyi'o//phyag rgya bcings pa tsam 
21b2(rgyud): gyis ni/bram ze'i cha lug+sa don yod zhag+sa//mthong bar 'gyur te the tsom med//mgrin sngon dang ni rta mgrin dang/de bzhin rgya mtsho'i gling dang ni/mog na po zhal ni bcu gcig dang/de bzhin zhal ni bzhi dag dang/
21b3(rgyud): mog na po 'jig+sa pa brgyad 'phrog dang/de bzhin 'jig+sa pa bsam mi khyab/snang ba sna tshogs gzug+sa dang ni/sgyu ma lta bu'i ding 'dzin dang/phyag rgya bcings pa tsam gyis ni/mog na po thaMs cad ston par mdzad 
21b4(rgyud): bde ba can gyi 'jig rten khams/rgyal ba 'dren pa 'od dpag med//byang chub seMs rba'i tshogs bskor ba//mgon po mod la mod la mthong bar 'gyur gzhan yang sangs rgyas zhing ni stong 
21b5(rgyud): dag dang/bya chu bo ga dga'i bye ma dang/mnyam pa'i de bzhin gshegs pa 'bum//bye ba brgya phrag stong rnaMs la/byang chub seMs dpa'i tshegs bcas yongasu skor mgon phyag rgya bcings 
21b6(rgyud): pa tsam gyis mthong bar 'gyur//mthong ba chen po rnaM pa gzhan mthong ste/nyin gcig bzhin du byang chub snying po dang/chos kyi 'khor lo rdo rje gdan//de dag rmi lam snang bar mthong bar 'gyur/de la yid 
21b7(rgyud): gnyis so nyi the tsom med/sangs rgyas rnaMs kyis gshung ba 'am mtshams med lnga//mgon po de la gdug sems nyid dang ni/byang chub seMs dpa'i bar chad 'phags pa la//kha zer nyid dang dam chos
21b8(rgyud): slu ba dang/rgyal ba'i mnar med gzhol ba dang/mi bzad dmyal ba bcu drug gnas pa dang/bskal pa 'bum tsho thaMs cad skad cig grol/phyag rgya gsang sngags bzlas pa tsam gyis 'am/thos sam mthong
22a1(rgyud): @#//ngam ming ni bzung bas kyang/sdig pa de kun 'jig 'gyur the tshom med//don yod zhags snying phyag rgya mthong ba yang/'jig rten mgon dang mtshungs te khyab par 'phag+sa/sangs rgyas 'bum 
22a2(rgyud): phrag stong dang gang ga'i//bye ba brgya stong dag ni mthong bar 'gyur//byang chub snying por dgos pa thams cad dang/chos kyi 'khor lo rab tu bskor bar 'gyur//don yod zhags pa'i snying po phyag rgya dang/
22a3(rgyud): gsang sngags mthong na bsod naMs phung chen gyi//bsod nams phung po 'di dag 'phel bar 'gyur//de'i tshe yang rnam par 'phel bar 'gyur//de ni dpag tu med par so ma nyi med//de nas rigs
22a4(rgyud): gsang 'chang ba rtsang gyur nas/rtsang ma'i gos bag+so nas ni thog mar yang//yo byang bsgrub pa rtsang spyod pa rtsang//rtag tu yan lag spos ni sna tshogs dang//sna tshogs gdugs pas 
22a5(rgyud): rab tu bdug bgyis shing//dpung pa lag pa lha'i spos kyis byugs//rtsang ma'i sa phyogs rtsang ma'i gdan bshams par//dkyil mo dkrung bcas nas ni byaMs pa sems dang/snying rje'i lhag pa 
22a6(rgyud): bsam pa bsam pa de nyid mnyam gzhag nas/phyogs bcu'i de bzhin gshegs pa'i sangs rgyas dang/rtag tu 'jig rten dbang la phyag rgya zhing/rtag tu bla ma mchod nas chos la brtson shin tu rig pas 
22a7(rgyud): tshig gzhan mi smra par/shin tu rig pas bsrung ba'i cho ga bgyi//shin tu bkra shing bla ma de nyid gus/bden par smra zhing snyam par smra ba dang/sems can kun la snying rje'i bdag nyid can/lhan cig rjes mthun
22a8(rgyud): gyur nas de dag ni/phyag rgya 'di bcings par gyur te/lag pa nyi ma'i tshul du gzhag la sor mor rnaMs/mnyam par bcings te mthe chung zla ba'i tshul du snying khar gzhag go//phyag rgya 'di ni don yod pa'i 
22b1(rgyud): rgyal ba'i snying po'i rtsa ba'i phyag rgya ste/byang chub kyi snying po'i bar du rtsol bar bgyid do//mgon po 'jig rten dbang phyug dgug pa'i gsang sngags 'di brjod do//A+o~M a mo g+ha pa kur di bi ta sa man ta a   
22b2(rgyud): pa lo ka ya man te 'i yatA//a d+hi Sh+ThA ta a mo g+ha pad me tsa ra san tsa ra hUM huM/de ma thag tu dus gsum du rnam par lta ba don yod pad ma 'bar//gsal ba'i cha 'phrul dang bcas pa'i phyag rgya'i rgyal pos 
22b3(rgyud): shin du phra ba'i rdul dang mnyam pa'i dus gsuM du gsheg+sa pa'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing mang po mtha' dag cher g.yo bar 'gyuro//dus gsuM    du gshegs shing bzhugs pa'i sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das 
22b4(rgyud): de dag gi sku las 'od zer sna tshogs 'bum phrag brgya sdong du ma byung nas/de bzhin gsheg+sa pa de dag thaMs cad rab tu degs shing /sangs rgyas kyi yid la mdzad pa'i thugs rje chen pos 
22b5(rgyud): phyag gser gyi mdog lta bu rkyang nas/rigs sngag+sa 'chang gi spyi bor 'jog par mdzad do//grub pa dam pa don yod pa rnam par sprul pa'i byin gyis brlabs pa brtsol par 'gyur ro//dbang phyug 
22b6(rgyud): dang dbang phyug chen po dang/tshangs pa dang lha'i bu dang/gshin rje dang chu lha dang/lus ngan dang khyab bdag dang kun 'gyed dang/gzhon nu la sogs pa lha rnams bsrung ba dang/skyob ba dang sbed par bgyid par 
22b7(rgyud): 'gyur ro//phyag rgya bcings pa tsam gyi mdo la sdig pa dang sgrib pa thams cad dang 'jig+sa pa brgyad rnams dang/chab pa thams cad las yongasu thar par 'gyuro//de'i grub pa yid du 'chi bar 'gyuro//las
22b8(rgyud): thaMs cad bgyid pa'i phyag rgya thaMs cad du mchog las par grags pa lagaso//don yod dam tshig mchog grags pa/pad ma rgyas pa'i phyag rgya bcing/'dzub mo gong mo rnaMs pad ma lcags kyu'i tshul du bgyi//
23a1(rgyud): @#//sor mo mnyam par gzhag pa'i phyag rgya 'di stan te/rnam par grol ba'i dkyil 'khor rtsa ba'i phyag rgya 'dzin pa'i gsang sngags 'di brjod do//A+oM a mo g+ha pad ma gug sha sa ma ya na/gri b+ha gri b+ha/
23a2(rgyud): d+ha ra d+ha ra mahA sang tA hUM/phyag rgya bcings te bcings ma thag du 'am/bzung ba tsam gyis dus gsum du gsheg+sa pa'i sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnaMs kyis legaso zhes bgyi ba lasogs par 'gyur ro/
23a3(rgyud): de bzhin gshegs pa de dag thaMs cad dag+se pa dang /mnyes pa dang rab tu dges pa dang /tshim pa dang thugs dges pa skye bar 'gyur zhing ri gas sngags 'tshang la thugs rje chen po rgyas pa'i snying po grub pa dam
23a4(rgyud): pa rtsol bar 'gyur zhing /yang nas yang du mthong bar brtsol bar 'gyuro//mchod pa thaMs cad brtsol bar 'gyuro//don yod pa'i zhags pa'i dam tshig grub pa brtsol bar 'gyuro//de bzhin
23a5(rgyud): gshegs pa gsang ba'i dkyil 'khor gyi cho ga zhig mo'i phyag rgyad dang /gsang sngags sngags 'dzin pa rnam par grol ba'i dkyil 'khor bzung pa tsam gyis dam tshig rjesu 'jug par 'gyuro//thaMs
23a6(rgyud): cad du 'phags pa spyan ras gzigs dbang phyug gi gsang ba'i dkyil 'khor phyag rgya dang dam tshig 'tshal par 'gyuro//thams cad du dgongs pa'i gsang sngags grub par 'gyuro//'phag+sa
23a7(rgyud): pa spyan ras gzigs dbang d+yug mchog sbyin pa rtag tu lta bar 'tshal bar 'gyuro//lag 'thil pad ma ltar bgyis la 'dzub mo rje ltar bgyis zhing /mthe'u chung rkyang phyag ni/pad ma 'tshang la 'dug
23a8(rgyud): pa dang /me tog laswogs dri rnaMs dang /phreng ba byug pa phye ma dang /bdan gos dang rgyan dang ni/ma mchod yon rab tu dbul/buM pa gang ba dri swogs dang /mtshan yang zas ni sna tshogs la/phyag rgya

28b4(rgyud): bkug pa ni/kar shar chen mo phyag rgya ste/de ni kun grub bla na med/rdo rje'i tshul du thal sbyar dam bcing shing/shes rab can gyis g.yas pa'i lag sgyur la//khro gnyer bzhi ni de bzhin
28b5(rgyud): khro bo dang/phyag rgya chen po kun 'od rab tu sbyin//lag gnyis kyi thal sbyar nas/gsang ba de rkyang gung mo bkug nas su/mthe cung rkyang nas lcags kyu pad ma yi//tshul du snying kha'i gzhag
28b6(rgyud): pad par bya//phyag rgya 'di ni phyag rgya che//phyag rgya 'di ni bcings pa yis//thal sbyar dkyil 'khor kun nas lha dag dang/thaMs cad 'tshogs shing 'du ba dang/kun gyis bsrung bgyid so ma nyi med//phyag rgya
28b7(rgyud): 'di nyid bshig nas lag pa ni//thal sbyar pad ma'i tshul du bya/snying khar gzhag nas gnod sbyin pho nya ma'i//'di ni phyag rgya lag pas legs par bsgrags//dang po rdo rje khu tshur legs bcings la/
28b8(rgyud): gung mo lhag ma skum zhing mthe pong gzhag/pad ma'i tshul du bzhin dran sgrol bdud kyi/grub pa kun gyi bla med phyag rgya 'di/sdig pa thaMs cad 'jig cing nyon mongs kun/nad kun nye bar zhi bar som nyi med
29a1(rgyud): @#//don yod kun grub pa'i bla med kyi/cho ga zhib mo thaMs cad rab tu 'grub//lag pa gnyis ni mnyam gzhag ste/rdo rje'i tshul du pad ma bcing/'dzub mo bkug pa bgyis ni/mthe bong yang
29a2(rgyud): ni dgug pa rab gyi//'di ni rta mgrin phyag rgya ste/rgya mtsho klong gi kha lta bu/rdo rje za ba nyid kyis gsungs/'di ni las kun bgyid pa po//grub pa kun gyi bla na med/phyag rgya 'di ni 'dzub mo'i mthe 
29a3(rgyud): bong gnyis//lcags sgrog lhag ma skums n+sa pad ma'i tshul/snying kha gnasu gzhag par mgrin sngon gyi//'di ni dgos med las kyi phyag rgya lags//laganyis pad ma'i khu tshur te/g.yas g.yon
29a4(rgyud): du ni gzhag gnasu kun nas zhi ba'i gzhin du gzhag/byaMs pa'i phyag gnyis bgyid pa ni/zhal ni bcu gcig phyag rgya ste/de bzhin kun gyis mi thub pa'o//mthe bong mnyam par 
29a5(rgyud): mnyam bgyis pa/ser mo rtse mo skum pa dang/'dzub mo gnyis ni pad ma'i tshul/rig+sa pa'i dbang phyug phyag rgya lags/phyag rgya 'di nyid dbus su ni 'khor lo tshul du 'dzub mo skum/g.yon
29a6(rgyud): pa rkyang dbang chen gyi/phyag rgya 'byung po thaMs cad 'joms//lcags dam du bgyis nas ni/lag pa gnyis pa rkyang nas ni/snying khar gzhag pa'i phyag rgya ste/'di ni rgyun du gshin rje lags lag pa g.yon pa 
29a7(rgyud): rkyang nas su/sor mo sgreng ba'i thong to dgug/g.yas pa snying khar gzhag nasu/mthe bong dang ni mthe chung dgug/lhag ma rnaMs ni rkyang bgyis te/phyag rgya 'di ni lha'i phyag rgya ste/klu kun dgug pa'i mchog
29a8(rgyud): lagaso//lag pa gnyis ni sngams bgyis la//sor mo phyin ci log tu sgrel/g.yas pa dang ni g.yon pa dag/snying gi gnasu gzhag bgyis ste/phyag rgya 'di ni lus ngan gyi/gnod sbyin thaMs cad
29b1(rgyud): skrag par bgyid/lag pa g.yas pa spyi bor yang /mthe bong 'dzub mo dgug bgyis pa/lag pa g.yon pa rkyang nasu /'dzub mo gyen du sgreng ba ni/'di ni tshangs pa'i phyag rgya ste/thaMs cad du ni las 
29b2(rgyud): bsgrub pa'i//phyag rgya 'di nyid g.yas pa yi//mthe chung sor mo bkug byas te/drang srong tshogs kyi phyag rgya 'di lha rnaMs kun gyis phyag bgyis pa'i/lag pa gnyis ni dam bcing pa/lha sbug 'khor
29b3(rgyud): lo'i phyag rgya ste/lha men thaMs cad 'gum pa'i/khyab 'jug phyag rgya 'di zhes gsungs//g.yas pa'i bu dza bu rang bgyi/g.yon pa rtse gsum tshul du bgyi/mthe bo mthe chung skums pa ni/'byung po 
29b4(rgyud): thaMs cad skrag bgyi pa/'di ni drag po'i phyag rgyar gsungs/g.yas pa khu tshur bcangs nas su/'dzub mo rkyang nas g.yon pa ni/bkur sten khu tshur bcangs nas gzhag/sor
29b5(rgyud): mo rnaMs ni rkyang par bgyi/dran po rgyun du lta bgyi pa/de bzhin lta'i deng dpon gyi/gzhon nu phyag rgya 'di nyi zhes gsungaso;gyen du lag pa gnyis rkyang nas/sor mo gsum ni rkyang bgyis 
29b6(rgyud): te;mthe bo 'dzub mo bkug pa ni/'od zer stong dag 'phro bgyid pa/'di ni nyi ma'i phyag rgya lags/phyag rgya 'di nyid sor mo gsum/bkug nas sor ni gnyis gnyis rkyang /skar ma'i tshogs kyis 
29b7(rgyud): 'dzes gyur pa/'di ni zla ba'i phyag rgyar gsungs/gsang sngags chen po phyag 'di dag ni/las dang don kun grub pas bsgrub par bgyi/sdig pa thaMs cad spong zhing nyon mongs kun/nyams 
29b8(rgyud): par bgyid cing sgrib pa thaMs cad dag/las kun zad par bgyid cing don yod pa'i/las kun don yod grub pa 'gyur/A+o~M pad ma ta re ta ra ya/pad ma bud tsa hU~M/A+o~M mahA pad me shed drang ge/hu ru 
30a1(rgyud): @#//hu ru swAhA/A+o~M pri kun ti pad me/ta ra ta ra hU~M/A+o~M pad me ban di ra ba si ni/kun ta kun ta swahA//A+o~M a mo g+ha pad ma par te/sid kya sid d+ha du d+h+yaM/A+o~M sarba tra ga mi ni a pa ta ya re'u dri ni sUM/
30a2(rgyud): a mo g+ha ni ta ri ta ra ya pad ma pu tsa hUM/A+o~M ta ru ta ru la pad me a mo g+ha hUM/A+o~M pa ta pa mu khye ni krin ta ya du stan hUM/A+o~M pad me ni la kan te sho rwa/bu ru bu ru hUM/A+o~M se'u mya pa ta ni pra si ta sha si ya hUM/A+o~M pad me
30a3(rgyud): shwa ra sa ta ya hUM/A+o~M sa mi ta ra ta re hUM/ya ya sha ti pa ti mo ru mo ru hUM/a mo g+ha bri mar te hUM/A+o~M shwa ra bar te/a mo g+ha pu tse sU~M/A+o~M a mo g+ha ra tsa pad man b+he/sa ra sa ra sa men ta bu tse swAhA/A+o~M ru dra ru dri mi
30a4(rgyud): li swAhA/A+o~M shwa ka ti da ri a mo g+ha b+ha ti swAhA/A+o~M swa hA k+Shi/ri ni hUM/du ra ni/hUM du re swAhA/A+o~M swam bra g+he hUM//phyag rgya tha dad/gsang sngags 'chang gis ci skad gsungs pa'i las/rtag tu kun
30a5(rgyud): bgyid 'dor na thaMs cad bgyid/rtsad ma spyod pa rtsing ma dag/legs bkrus rtsad ma'i gos dgos na dri zhim dri ma yis dpung pa dang /lag pa yan lag byugs nas su/phyag rgya lan re bcings pa na//
30a6(rgyud): gsang sngags lan re bzlas par bgyi//phyag rgya bdag ni bcings pa dang /gsang sngags bzlas pa'i mod la ni//brgya stong ni 'bum brag 'bum/phyag rgya dag ni bcings par 'gyur ba na//bsod naMs kyi phung
30a7(rgyud): po chen po dang ldan par 'gyuro//sangs rgyas dang byang chub seMs dpa' thaMs cad kyis lus bstan pa dang /byin gyis rlabs par 'gyuro//lha thaMs cad kyis legs par bsrungs bar 'gyuro//'jig rten thaMs cad
30a8(rgyud): kyi mchod gnas gcig pur 'gyuro//'jig rten thaMs cad kyi bla ma gcig pur 'gyuro//dkyil 'khor gyi phyag rgya'i chog rim par phye ba dam tshig thaMs cad la zhugs par 'gyuro//tshe dpag tu ma mchis par 'gyuro/
30b1(rgyud): don yod pa'i rigasngags 'chang 'grub par 'gyuro//tshig btsan par 'gyuro//dran pa dang blo gros bag tsha ba ma mchis par 'gyuro//blo gros thog+sa pa med par 'gyuro//'phag+sa pa spyan ras gzig+sa dbang phyug mchog
30b2(rgyud): rtag tu 'tshal bar 'gyuro//rtag tu dam pa brtsol bar 'gyuro//rtag tu  dgra dang pas kyi rgol ba dang /mi 'dza' ba dang /bdug pa dang bar du bdag pa thaMs cad pham par bgyid par 'gyuro//rtag tu thas cad du rgyal
30b3(rgyud): bar 'gyuro//gnod sbyin dang /srin po dang /'byung po dang /sha za dang /bgeg+sa dang /log 'dren dang /lha ma yin dang /klu thaMs cad mthong bar 'gyuro//'phag+sa pa chen po thaMs cad la bag tshe ba ma mchis par 'gyuro
30b4(rgyud): rgyal po dang blon po dang btsun mo'i 'khor dang /bcas pa thaMs cad dbang du gyur nas mchis par 'gyuro//thaMs cad du gtso bo gcig bur 'gyuro//thaMs cad du bla ma'i gcig pur 'gyuro/
30b5(rgyud): tshe ring bar 'gyuro//nad thaMs cad las thar par 'gyuro//skyon gyi dri ma dang ser sna dang /'phra dog thaMs cad dang bral bar 'gyuro//legs par dran cing lus shin tu yongasu dag pa 'gyuro
30b6(rgyud): 'chi ba'i dus kyi mod la de bzhin gsheg+sa pa mthong bar 'gyuro//'phag+sa pa spyan ras gzig+sa dbang phyug mthong bar 'gyur zhing de dag thaMs cad kyi dbug+sa 'byin par 'gyuro//rigs kyi tshur shog/'jig 
30b7(rgyud): rten gyi khaMs bde bar can du song shig/ces shin tu dran pa nye bar gzhag nas//dus kyid par 'gyuro//dmyal ba'i sgo thaMs cad bcad par 'gyuro//ngan song gi sgo thaMs cad las yongasu grol bar 'gyuro/
30b8(rgyud): 'di nas ma 'ongs pa mang du skye bar 'gyuro//skye ba rtag tu skye ba dran par 'gyuro//thaMs cad du pad ma la rdzus te skye bar 'gyuro//byang chub kyi snying po la thug gi bar du /sangs rgyas kyi zhing nas sangs 
31a1(rgyud): @#//rgyas kyi zhing du skye bar 'gyuro//de nas 'jig rten dbang phyug sprul pa yi//phyag rgya'i rtag+sa ni brjod par bgyi//khru gang kho ni' tshad dag gis/dkyil 'khor lhag ma dri bar bya/gang gis mthong 
31a2(rgyud): ba'i mdo tsam nas/dga' ba dang ni mgu bar 'gyur//sdig pa kun las grol ba dang/de nyid bdag ni 'grub par 'gyur//rgung du gang gi cho ga yi//rtag tu go cha dgos par 'gyur//go cha de ni 'bar ba dang/
31a3(rgyud): mdzes pa rtag tu brtsol bar 'gyur/cho ga zhib mo legs 'grub ste/gsang sngags bla med thaMs cad 'grub//phyag rgya'i dkyil 'khor bris tsam gyis//gnod sbyin srin po 'byer bar 'gyur//mthong 
31a4(rgyud): ba tsam gyis don kun 'grub//'jig par 'gyur te som nyi med/gnod pa kun las grol 'gyur te/nad kun las kyang de bzhin te/pha rol dpung ngam rang bsrung ngam/mi thub pa'i dgra

31a6(rgyud): 'gyur zhing/'phags pa spyan ras gzigs dbang phyug gis//phyag rgya'i mtshan ma mtshon pa dang/dgos pa kun gyi rjes 'thun dang//gang dang gang du bkod pa dang/de dang der ni phyir brgyar 'gyur/pad 
31a7(rgyud): ma kha phya zhes bgyi ba/pad ma'i 'dab mas mdzes pa dang/kun nas 'khor ba 'bar ba'i phreng//rnam par rtog pa med par bgyi/phyag rgya 'di kun srog las byung/'di dag thaMs cad 'bras bu rtsol/rdo rje 
31a8(rgyud): rtse gsum rdo rje'i mda'/dkyil 'khor pad ma rdo rje dang/pad ma'i phreng ba rdo rje'i mtshan/pad ma rab gnas pad ma gnyis/rtag tu pad pa legs par bgyis/pad ma'i khu tshur de bzhin du/pad 'dab 
31b1(rgyud): ma pad mas sgyings/pad ma'i lcags kyu thar mo sbyar//pad ma'i lcags sgrog pad ma'i gzer/rtag tu pad ma sems dpa' dang/nor bu pad ma rdo rjer gsungs/don yod pad ma de bzhin du/pad ma'i
31b2(rgyud): ral gri pad ma dril/rtse gsuM zhes bgyi rtun shing 'chang//nor bu dbyug pa me tog dbyug/de bzhin gzhags pa rtun shing dang//chu srin 'dzin dang 'khor lo phyed//tsa pi tsi pu ra rtag tu se'u /zla 'dzin nyi 
31b3(rgyud): ma 'dzin pa dang//mthe bo 'dzin dang tshe mas shing dang//me tog pur ma bgrang phreng dang//rdo rje rtse lnga don yod zhags//don yod grub pa de dag ste//thams cad pad ma'i steng du bkod//lag pa'i phyag
31b4(rgyud): rgya 'di 'bri pa;gang dang gang du bkod pa ni//lag pa'i phyag rgya bzhi bcu tham//'di dag kyang ni bri bar bya//kun nas kyang ni ;'bar bar bgyi;nor bu'i rgyan gyis blubs par bgyi//dkyil

31b6(rgyud): gnasu rtog+sa pa dang/mtshams med pa ni lnga nyid dang//thas cad skad cig tsam gyis byang//sems can dag pa dran bgyid ni//phyir mi ldog pa nyid du 'gyur//sems dpe' sa bcur rab tu gnas//'di ni phyir mi 
31b7(rgyud): ldog pa yi//gnas lags dkyil 'khor mthong ba ni//'phags pa spyan ras gzig+sa dbang mchog sbyin gyi/rtag par rgyun du spyi bo lag na thogs gang gis phyag rgya 'di mthong ba//rgyun du mchog sbyin
31b8(rgyud): 'jig rten dbang//rtag du mngon sum mthong ba yi//de dag mtshungs te khyad par 'phags//gsang sngags sum bcu rtsa lnga'i tshig rtag tu dran bgyis thams cad 'grub//de'i dus rnams kun tu yang 
32a1(rgyud): //ngan 'gror 'chi bar mi 'gyuro//rnam grol dkyil 'khor rab myur ba//myur du sangs rgyas nyid thob 'gyur//'od dpag med pa'i sras po yi//thu bor gyur pa som nyi med//'jig
32a2(rgyud): rten mgon gyi thugs zhal nas//skye sras rtag tu thub par 'gyur//'dren pas 'di ni chos nyid dang //chos las skyes par lung ston 'gyur//gang ga'i klung bye ma brgya stong dang/mnyam pa'i bye ma snyed
32a3(rgyud): dag gi/de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rnaMs la dge ba'i rtsa bskyed par 'gyuro//tshad med grangs med dpag tu med pa'i bsod naMs kyi phung po thob
32a4(rgyud): par 'gyuro//gang gis gus par phyag rgya dang//gsang sngags tshig 'di mchis gyur na//phyag rgya 'di las de lta bu'i phan yon mang zhing che ba mchis pa sgragaso//de la gsang sngags ni/
32a5(rgyud): A+oM a ma g+ha dza mi li dza la hUM/A+oM a shwa sa ya k+Sha ni pa ga ti hUM/A+oM a pa ya bi ra te ti hUM/A+oM sarba ta thA ga ta a d+hi sti te/d+hi ri d+hi ri hUM/A+oM pad mo hI te ya ma shi hUM/A+oM pi sa ra pi ras tsa ma lan ta ra
32a6(rgyud): yang hUM/A+oM a sha g+hu sha a mo sha ya hUM//A+o ka pa tsa ya du ru du ru hUM/A+oM a mo g+ha shang ka la mi la mi li hUM/A+oM a mo ga tsi Ta man ni du ti ru ru hUM/A+oM a mo g+ha ma ni tsi li tsi li hUM/A+oM badzra du ti tsi li hUM/A+oMa

32b1(rgyud): shuli pa sha pi lo ka ya ta ra ya hUM//A+oMn ga pa sha pa na d+ha kang d+ha du Sh+Ta na gan pa sha pa ta ya hUM/A+oM dza long pa bA da ra yA hUM/A+oM tsan tra bi ma le/b+ha shi d+ha ya an dkar/ra sh+m+yi pi re hUM/A+oM sra ra bo ta ya hUM/

33b3(rgyud): phyag rgya dang ni gsang sngags dam pa dag/'di 'chang ba la myur du 'gyur//de nas don yod pa las thaMs cad pa dang rjesu mthun pa'i zhags pa bdun bshad par bgyi ste/de nyid bsgrubs pas ji lta bu'i

103'b6(rgyud): de nas mi smra bar dkyil mo drung du 'dug ste zlas rjod kyi phyag rgya bcings la/don yod pa'i zhags pa' snying po lan brgya rtsa brgyad zlas par bya'o/de nas de'i 'og tu don yod grub pa'i rigasngags kyi rgyal po stong rtsa brgyad zlas
103'b7(rgyud): par bya/de nas ras kyi sku gzugs la 'od zer bdun byung nas/kun nas snang bar byas te lan gsum skor ba byas te/'phags pa spyan ras zigasadabang phyug spyan drang zhugs par 'gyur/zhugs pa tsam gyi mod
103'b8(rgyud): la sa cher g.yos par 'gyur zhing /rigasngags 'chang yang snying dga' zhing ba spu ldang bar 'gyur/mdog mdangs yongasu dag par 'gyur/'phags pa spyan ras gzigs dbang phyug gi mchod sbyin gyi phyag 
104'a1(rgyud): @#//rgya sor mo las;lha'i chu 'dzag par 'gyur//zhabs gnyis las 'o ma 'dzag par 'gyur//don yod pa'i zhabs pa'i rgya phyag rgya 'bar bar 'gyur;phyag na pa'i lo ma g.yo bar
104'a2(rgyud): 'gyur;rtse gsum g.yo bar 'gyur//zhing//legs so zhes bya ba'i sgra 'byung bar 'gyur te//rigs kyi bu legs so legs so/khyod kyis don yod pa'i zhabs pa'i snying por grub par gyis;las thams
104'a3(rgyud): cad byos shig/khyod kyis don yod grub pa'i cho ga zhib mo'i rgyal po dam pa grub pa zhes nas//de nas 'phags pa spyan ras gzigs dbang phyug gzugs stan par mdzad do/'dod pa thams cad kyi 
104'a4(rgyud): mchog sbyin par mdzad do/'di ni grub pa'i mchog bla na med pa ste/ma bsgrubs par las byed pa'o/de nas dbang du bya bar 'dod na/me tog pad ma la lan brgya rtsa brgyad bzlas la;'phags pa spyan ras
104'a5(rgyud): gzigs dbang phyag la phul te/gang ming nas brjod de dbang du 'gyur ro/sna'i me tog la lan brgya rtsa brgyad bzla te//'phags pa spyan ras gzig dbang phyug la phul la;yang lan bdun
104'a6(rgyud): bzlas nas;me tog snyim pa blangs te/gang la byin pa de dbang du 'gyuro;gdon thams cad las yongs su grol bar 'gyur ro nad kyis thebs pa la byin na/nad thams cad las grol bar 'gyuro
104'a7(rgyud): rgyal po'i sgor gtor na rgyal po'i btsun mo'i 'khor dang bcas pa dbang du 'gyur ro;mi ro sregs pa'i stengs su rtor nas;sems can de sems can dmyal ba las grol bar 'gyur ro;mnar med pa gnas
104'a8(rgyud): pa'i sems can dmyal ba rnams la rtor na/skad cig de las yongs su grol par 'gyur ro//nyal ba'i sngas su gzhag nas ma rmi lam chen po mthong bar 'gyur ro/'phags pa spyan ras gzi gas dbang phyug sku stonas par
104'b1(rgyud): mdzad cing;yid du 'ong ba thams cad gsung bar 'gyur ro/skyes pa 'am bud med gang gi steng du gtor ba de dbang du 'gyur zhing grib ma bzhin 'brang bar 'gyur ro/rgyal po'i steng du rtor na bran bzhin du 'gyur ro;lha'i
104'b2(rgyud): gzugs brnyan gyi steng du gzhag na 'gugs par 'gyur ro/las thams cad byed par 'gyur ro;mig sman la bsgrub par 'dod pas;ras kyi sku gzugs kyi spyan sngar mig sngan ldong ros la lan stong rtsa brgyad
104'b3(rgyud): bzlas te;grub pa'i rtags bcu ston par 'gyur te;'bar ba dang ;dbugs 'byung ba dang;dud pa 'byung bar 'gyur te;des mig la skus te snang bar 'gyur te;gter thams mthong bar 'gyur ro;
104'b4(rgyud): gnod sbyin gyi gnas thams cad mthong bar 'gyur ro;bral bar thig le byas na;'phags pa spyan ras gzigs dbang phug thams cad du 'jug par 'gyur ro;sgo thams cad dang;'khrul 'khor thams cad dbye bar 'gyuro
104'b5(rgyud): yan lag dang nying lag la skus na nam mkha' la 'khor bar 'gyur ro;dbye bor thig le byas na/'phags pa spyan ras gzig+sadbang phyug la gyis mchod sbyin par 'gyur ro;mig la byugs na/khyab 
104'b6(rgyud): 'jug dang dbang phyug chen po laswogs pa lha thams cad rtag tu phyi bzhin 'brang bar 'gyur zhing;dgos pa thams cad 'grub par byed do/mtshan mo mig la skus na/nyin bzhin du mthong bar 'gyur zhing;gter thams
104'b7(rgyud): cad 'byung bar 'gyur te/khas phul nas dor bar bya'o//rang gyi lus la byugs na stong 'tshe'i bar du go cha dgos pa'i lus su 'gyur ro;gnod sbyin dang srin po laswogs pas mi mthong bar 'gyur zhing;las thams cad byed par
104'b8(rgyud): 'gyur ro//tshe skal pa stong thub par 'gyur ro;bza' ba snying khar sku ba byas na/nag po chen po'i gzugs mthong bar 'gyur zhing;lha thams cad kyis gzugs stong par 'gyur nas;tshangs pa dang khyab 'jug dang dbang
105'a1(rgyud): @#//phyug chen po'i gzugs dang ;gzugs sna tshogs yongs su ston par 'gyur ro/zla ba dang nyi ma ston par 'gyur ro/ldong ros dang 'gyi wang dang;se'u bi ri ga dang;gur gum dang;ku ku pa la don yod par
105'a2(rgyud): grub pa khri bzlas;don yod par zhags pa'i snying po stong rtsa brgyad bzlas;khro bo'i rgyal po brgya rtsa brgyad byas te thams cad du mi smra bar byas shing;'phags pa spyan ras gzigs dbang phyug gyi spyan spang btags la ras kyi sku 
105'a3(rgyud): gzugs la mngon par phyogs te;rtsang mas mos rtsang ma gos la;chos bcwo pa nya la zhag cig stung ba byas nas phye ma zhib tu btags te;yang don yod pa'i zhags pa'i snying po dang;khro bo'i rgyal po dang;don yod pa'i grub pa'i 
105'a4(rgyud): rigs sngags kyi rgyal po brgyad bzlas brjod byas la;de'i 'og tu 'phags pa spyan ras gzigasadabang phyug gyi phyag tu gzhag ste;:mchod pa chen po byas nas ras kyi sku gzugs la mchod pa byas la nyin zhig cig smyung ba
105'a5(rgyud): byas te bzlas brjod kyi phyag rgya byas la;khro bo'i lan bdun bzlas te;de'i 'og tu mig la skus na/don yod pa'i grub pa'i gzungs bzlas pa byas na/skus pa tsam gyis 'phags pa spyan ras gzig dbang phyug
105'a6(rgyud): ri bo po ta la dang bcas gzhal med khang dang bcas;byang chub sems dpa'i tshogs gzhal med khang dang bca' pa;ri bo po ta la gnas pa mthong bar 'gyur ro//bde ba can gyi 'jig rten gyi khams mthong zhing 
105'a7(rgyud): de bzhin gshegs pa 'od dpag med chos 'chad pa mthong bar 'gyur ro//chos bshad pa de yang thob par 'gyur ro/phyogs bcu'i de bzhin gshegs pa byang chub sems dpa'i tshogs dang bcas ;nyan thos kyi tshogs dang;
105'a8(rgyud): bcas;sangs rgyas kyi zhing gzhal med khang bcas pa mthong bar 'gyur zhing;chos bshad pa'i gsung gyi sgra thos par 'gyur ro;'di ltar rigs ngan gyi rigs su skyes pa dang;mnar med par gzhol pa dang;
105'b1(rgyud): 'tshams med pa lnga byas pa gang yin pa de dag thams cad kyang de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyis byin gyis rlabs pa dang;'phags pa spyan ras gzigs dbang phyug gi thugs rjes dgongs par 'gyur zhing;rmi laM
105'b2(rgyud): du ston par mdzad do//sdig pa dang;sgrib pa thams cad rnam par dag par sbyong bar 'gyur;mchog thams cad sbyin par 'gyur ro;don yod pa'i zhags pa'i snying po dngos grub sbyin par 'gyur;lha'i lo stong tsho
105'b3(rgyud): bar 'gyur zhing lha'i bde ba nyams su myong bar 'gyur;nad kyis thebs pa thams cad la mig sman 'di skus na/nad thams cad zhi bar 'gyur zhing;rims nad zhi bar 'gyur ro;dug thams cad la skus pa tsam gyis
105'b4(rgyud): dug med par 'gyuro;'jigs pa thams cad la mig la skus na/mimthong bar 'gyur;phyir dgol ba dang dgra dang;g.yo can;dang chom po dang;rkun po thams cad kyis mi mthong bar 'gyur;
105'b5(rgyud): ma tshon gyi 'jigs par mi 'gyur ro;'thab pa dang rtsod pa dang;khrag'khrug pa la mig la skus na/thams cad du rgyal zhing lan thebs par 'gyur ro;g.yul po thams can du mig la skus na/
105'b6(rgyud): g.yul 'gyed pa la rgyal bar 'gyur zhing/dpung gi tshogs las rgyal bar 'gyur ro//rgyal po'i pho brang du 'gro ba'i tshe mig la skus na/rgyal po'i btsun mo 'khor dang bcas pa dbang du 'gyur ro//mi smra bar 
105'b7(rgyud): grong khyer du song na/skyes pa dang;bud med dang;khye'u dang bu mo grong khyer du gnas so;mchog thams cad dbang du 'gyur ro/mi ga g.yas pa la skus na;rigs bzhi'i 'jig rten gang mthong ba des 
105'b8(rgyud): thams cad du 'gyur ro;mig g.yon pa la skus na/rigs ngan thams cad du 'gyur ro;'phags pa spyan ras gzigs dbang phyug gyi phyag g.yas pa nas;gzung ste/don yod pa'i grub pa'i rigs
106'a1(rgyud): //sngags kyi rgyal po lan stong rtsa brgyad bzlas brjod byas te 'phags pa spyan snga gzigs dbang phyug la mchod pa byas na/de nas don yod pa'i zhags pa'i snying po dang;khro bo'i rgyal po dang;
106'a2(rgyud): gsungs thams cad so sor lan stong phrag brgya bzlas par 'gyur ro;de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas bye ba stong phrag bcu'i drung du dge ba'i rtsa ba bskyed par 'gyur;
106'a3(rgyud): thams cad kyi ming bzung bar 'gyur;de bzhin gshegs pa stong mnyes pa byas par 'gyur;de bzhin gshegs;pa stong phrag drug bcu rtsa bzhi mngon sum du mthong bar 'gyur;'jig rten pa'i don thams cad 
106'a4(rgyud): 'grub par 'gyur;sngon gyi sgrib pas mnar med pa'i gnas su 'gro ba yongs su dag par 'gyur;nams kyang ngan song du 'gro bar 'gyur ro//tshe rabs tshe rabs su skye ba dran par 'gyur;lha thams
106'a5(rgyud): cad kyang mchog sbyin par 'gyur zhing;rtag tu bsrung ba dang skyab pa dang /sba bar byed par 'gyur ro;phyag g.yon pa gnan te lan stong rtsa brgyad bzlas brtsod byas na/bzlas pa tsam gyi 
106'a6(rgyud): mod la lha dang klu dang gnod sbyin dang;srin po la swogs par 'gyur zhing;thams cad dbang du 'gyur bar gnas so/gang gi tshe dgos par 'gyur ba de'i tshe/don yod par grub pa de'i rigs sngags kyi
106'a7(rgyud): rgyal po lan bdun bzlas pa byas na/lha dang klu laswogs pa thams cad kyang las thams cad byed cing rtag tu rgyun mi 'chad par phyi bzhin 'brang bar 'gyur ro/lte ba'i gnas du gnan te//bzlas na klu laswogs pa
106'a8(rgyud): thams cad char gyi rgyun 'bebs par 'gyuro//'dzam pa'i gling mtha' dag tshim pa byed par 'gyuro snyam du lan brgyad rtsa brgyad bzlas na/gang dang gang du song ba der skyes pa dang bud med dang;rgyal po dang blon
106'b1(rgyud): po dang/dge slong ngam;bram ze 'am;dge snyen nam/dge snyen ma la gang la bsams pa dang;gang bcol ba dang/gang smras pa'i las de thams cad grub par 'gyur ro//dbugs nas bzung ste//bzlas na/tshangs
106'b2(rgyud): pa 'jig rten gyi khams kyi bar du byas par 'gyur te/thams cad la dbang skur zhing sgo ba bzhin byed par 'gyur ro//thog mar bzlas brjod stong rtsa brgyad byas nas bzlas par bya'o//zhabs gnyis gnan te stong phrag
106'b3(rgyud): bcu bzlas pa byas na sa'i 'og yan chad dbang du byas par 'gyur zhing las thams cad byed par 'gyur te/mngag gzhug thams cad byed par 'gyuraro/pad ma la stong rtsa brgyad bzlas nas/pad ma'i mtshe'ur bo ba na/
106'b4(rgyud): pad ma'i zad mi shes par 'byung zhing /pad ma dus thams cad par 'gyur ro/ud pa dang la la la na stong rtsa brgyad bzlas nas;ud pa lala'i mtshe'ur bor na/ud pa la la sngon po zad mi shes pa
106'b5(rgyud): 'byung 'gyur zhing /nams kyang nyams par mi 'gyur te/dus thams cad du me tog 'byung bar 'gyur ro//de bzhin du sna ma'i me tog dang/gnad sa shi ka dang/yu ti ka dang/na ma li ka'i me tog
106'b6(rgyud): rnams kyang //dus thams cad du me tog 'byung bar 'gyurazhing /zad mi shes par 'gyur ro 'bras bu dang me tog la lan brgya rtsa brgyad bzlas te//skyed mos tshal lam //nags tshal gyi nang du gtor na me tog
106'b7(rgyud): dang 'bras bu zad mi shid dus thams cad par 'gyuraro//dpal chen po bkug par 'dod pas//sra tshil las dpal chen po'i dkyil 'khor mo dkrung byed du 'dug la rgyan thams cad bas brgyan pa;lus 'dud 
106'b8(rgyud): pa lag na pad ma thogs pa brad rgyas pa byas la 'bras bu dang me tog 'gram par bya zhing //ga pur dang bla rtsi dang//tsan dan dkar pos mchod pa byas la;ras kyi sku gzugs kyi spyan rasngar gzhag par bya zhing //
107'a1(rgyud): @#//sngar tsan dan dkar po las byas pa de dkyil 'khor cho ga bzhin byas la;'phags pa spyan ras gzigs dbang phyug ras kyi sku gzugs la;a ka ru'i bdug pas 'dul bar bya /dpal
107'a2(rgyud): lha mo la tsan dan dkar po'i bdug pa dbul bar byas zhing;gnyi ka la mchod pa dang snyen bkur bya;rtor ma dkar mo dang;dri chab dbul bar bya//mchod yon sna tshogs dbul bar bya ste//rigs sngags
107'a3(rgyud): 'chang rtsang mas dus gsum du khrus bya zhing;gos rtsang ma bgos pas mngon du phyogs te//dkyil mo dkrung du 'dug nas;don yod pa'i zhags pa'i phyag rgya lan bdun byas la;ji snyed pa'i dri dang phreng
107'a4(rgyud): ba dang;me tog la don yod pa'i zhags pa'i snying po lan brgya rtsa brgyad bzlas te//dpal lha mo chen mo'i matong la btags par bya'o;me tog gzhan gyis mchod pa bya zhing /tsan dan dkar
107'a5(rgyud): po la lan brgya rtsa brgyad pa'i bzlas pa'i bdug spos 'bul bar bya'o;de nas rigs sngags 'chang gis don yod pa'i snying po lan stong rtsa brgyad bzlas te/par bya zhing //yungs dkar 
107'a6(rgyud): re re la bzlas nas;dpal lha mo la bsnun par bya ste//stong rtsa brgyad tshar ba dang//dpal lha mo chen mo 'bar bar 'gyur ro//'phags pa spyan ras gzigs dbang phyug gi bral ba nas 'od zer 'gyuro
107'a7(rgyud): dkyil 'khor gyi yo byad gang yin pa thams cad gser du snang bar 'gyur ro//dpal lha mo chen mo dgug par 'gyur ro;zhing;ji snyed mtsho'i bar du bda' bar mi 'gyur ro//lha'i dpal rjesu
107'a8(rgyud): spyod par 'dod na/nyin re bzhin 'phags pa spyan ras gzigasadabang phyug dang/dpal lha mo chen po dus mchod par bya/zhing ;yod yon gyi snod kyang gzhag par bya zhing/dkon mchog gsu
107'b1(rgyud): la mchod par bya /sbyin par bya longs spyod par bya;bsod nams kyang bya zhing;dpal lha mo tshan mo dus gsum du mchod par bya/dpal lha mo rang gi gzugs kyis gnas par 'dod pas;don yod
107'b2(rgyud): pa'i rgyal po'i khro'i ba'i snying po stong rtsa brgyad bzlas par bya zhing;tsan dan kyi ri lu stong rtsa brgyad kyis 'dug par bya;yungs dkar gyis stong rtsa brgyad kyis bsnun par bya ste /dpal lha mo chen mo
107'b3(rgyud): brgyan pa thams cad kyis brgyan pa;lha'i nor bu dor gsor dang mu tig gis brgyan pa;lus snang gsal zhal pad ma kha phyi blta bu;spyan ud pa la ston po lta bur gsal la pad pa snams pa;
107'b4(rgyud): gdan pad ma nor bu dang;rin po che na dang;gser dang/pad ma'i char 'bebs pa;rin po che 'bar ba'i bum pa ston phrag thog+sa pa;lha'i bu mo dang/gnod sbyin gyi bu mo brgya stong
107'b5(rgyud): mang po thams cad kyis brgyan pas//lus la zhing 'bar zhing 'dzes pa mang mos yongs su bskor nas 'dun na 'khod bar 'gyur te/rigs sngags 'bang gis 'jigs par mi bya zhing/sems
107'b6(rgyud): can dga' bar byas la//gsang sngags 'di lan bdun bzlas par byas 'o//na mo rad na tra ya yA;na mo a r+ya lo gi ti shwa ra ya;bod de sad dwa yA;mahA kA ru ni ka yA//mahA a mo ga pha ra d+ha yA//
107'b7(rgyud): t+yad ya thA ga;A+o pad ma ni/pad ma ni tra//pad ma ra ge//pad ma da ri/pad ma ba ti//pad ma sre ye;pad ma bra b+he//pad ma g+he'u re//pad ma sa na pra ti sh+ti te//a ba lo gi ti/sri ye ba ra te ba ra de yi/
107'b8(rgyud): ni pra sri ti pra si da ya//ku ru ku ru;a r+ya pa lo ki /a r+ya lo gi ti ba ra te//tu ru tu ru//ba ra gi ni/tsa la la;pad ma bi su shi ti//pad ma sri ye;bi tsi tA //bi ma na da re//ba ra ba ra/sma ra
108'a1(rgyud): @#////ra sa ma yA//ar+ya pa lo ki ti shwa ra yA//sa me sad t+ya/a ti sh+sha na/de bi a mo ba sha /ba ra de hUM/na mo stu te swA hA//lan bdun bzlas pa tsam gyis//lha mo dpal chen po
108'a2(rgyud): mos phyag rkyang ste//rigs sngags 'chang gyi sbyin bor 'jog par 'gyur zhing /'di skad ces kyang gsung par 'gyur ro//rigs kyi bu chur shog//thugs las bskyed pa'i gsung las skyes pa//
108'a3(rgyud): khyod kyis don yod par zhags pa'i snying po khro bo dang bcas pa grub kyis;don yod pa'i zhags pa'i pad pa ma dri ma mod pa'i dpal zhes bya ba'i gzungs la zhugs shig/rang gyi gnas la rang gi//
108'a4(rgyud): khyim bzhin du las su bya ba thams cad byos shig/de nas rigs sngags 'chang gyis yang rigs sngags dang gsang sngags 'di lan gsum bzlas brjod bya'o//na mo rad na tra yA yA;na ma ar+ya
108'a5(rgyud): pa lo ki ti shwa ra yA;bod ti sad ti yA//mahA sad twa yA//mahA ka ru ni ka yA//oM ba gi ba ti/mahA dza de tra//ba ra bar/a ba lo ki ti pa ra te//k+khi ri ni sra pa tu//am pa am pa//pi bu
108'a6(rgyud): la ki d+hi ka re pra pa ra pu tse//a mo lo ki sra sha hA ste ha swA hA//de'o 'og tu dpa' la chen mo'i nub ma gnyis las 'o ma'i rgyun 'byung bar 'gyur ro//'o ma'i rgyun de 'thungs pa tsam gyi mod la//lha'i me tog pad
108'a7(rgyud): ma dang //ku mu ta ltar mdzes pa'i lha'i lus su gyur;skra cung zad 'khyil zhing 'khril//mig ud pa la sdong po lta bu dang //tshig gsal zhing mi 'am ci'i sgra dbyangs dang ldan par 'gyur ro//don yod
108'a8(rgyud): pa'i rgya mtsho nor bu chen po'i pad ma la rnam par lta ba'i dpal dri mi med pa zhes bya ba'i cho ga zhib mo'i rgyal po mngon sum tu 'gyur;cho ga zhib mo mang po 'grub par 'gyur//'tshe lo stong phrag bcu thub 
108'b1(rgyud): par 'gyur //rgyal ba 'od dpag med byang chub sems dpa'i tshogs dang thams gcig tu me gzhal med khang mchog na chos ston pa mthong bar 'gyur /rdzu 'phrul gyis nam mkha' la 'gro bar 'gyur//
108'b2(rgyud): nor bu chen po pad ma lta ba zhes bya ba'i don yod pa' zhags pa chen po'i 'khor la sgyur bar 'gyur//rigs sngags 'chang thams cad kyi 'khor lo bsgyur ba'i gtso bo gcig par 'gyur|'phags pa
108'b3(rgyud): pa spyan ras gzigs dbang phyug gi sras su 'gyur//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi sras gcig pur 'gyur|lha dang klu dang gnod sbyin dang dri za dang lha ma yin dang /nam mkha' lding dang//mi
108'b4(rgyud): 'am ci dang//lto 'phye chen po rnams kyi bu'i gtso bo gcig pur 'gyur|rgyal po chen po bzhi'i bu gcig par 'gyur //thams cad du stob+sadang pha rol gnon pa dang//brtson 'grus thogs pa med
108'b5(rgyud): pa dang ldan par 'gyur//de bzhin gshegasapa thams cad kyi phyag rgya dang dkyil 'khor rim par phye ba//rigs sngags kyi rgyal po gsang sngag+sapa'i gzungs kyi rgyal po cho ga zhib mo zab mo dag mngon
108'b6(rgyud): suM du 'gyur|/lha thams cad kyi gan+sa dang klu thams cad kyi gnas dang mi 'am ci thams cad kyi gnas dang//phug dang khrus kyi gnas thams cad sgo 'bye bar 'gyur ro//gnod sbyin dang srin po'i gnas thaMs cad
108'b7(rgyud): sgo 'bye bar 'gyur ro|/thaMs cad dbang du 'gyur zhing /bgyi ba ci mchis zhes bya ba byed cing 'dug par 'gyur//'jig rten thams cad kyi ston pa bla ma ci bu 'jig rten mgon po 'dra ba mchod pa'i gnas su
108'b8(rgyud): 'gyur|'jig rten pa dang 'jig rten las 'das pa'i gsang sngags dang dkyil 'khor thams cad grub pa chen por 'gyur//dpal chen po 'khor dang//bcas pa la sbyin sregs bgyis na /gsang sngag+sa
109'a1(rgyud): @#//'chang de la bu bzhin byams pas|rang nyid bsnyen bkur yongs su dpyad pa(sa) yongs su spyod do//'dod pa thams cad dang yid du 'ong ba thams cad kyi dgos pa byed par 'gyur te nor bu
109'a2(rgyud): dang 'bru dang //dbyig dang gser dang nor bu dang/mu tig dang/bye du r+ya dang/shang ka shi la dang/bye ru dang/sa le sbram dang//gdul dang//lha'i nor bu ni(sa) po che'i bar thams cad du sbyin par byedado//yul
109'a3(rgyud): ka shi ka'i gos bzang po mang mo sbyin par byed cing//lha'i na bza' dang/btung ba'i bar dang//bdud rtsi'i ro' bar du sbyin par byed do//gnas thams cad du song ba na yang rtag tu rgyun bu la byaMs
109'a4(rgyud): pa'i tshul gyis rtag tu phyi bzhin 'brang zhing 'gro bar 'gyuro//rigs sngags 'tshang rang gi khyim yang rgyal po'i pho brang lcang lo can ji lta ba bzhin du rigs sngags 'chang gi gzhal med
109'a5(rgyud): khang mchog tu 'gyuro//rnam thos kyi(bu) mo'i gnas na 'byor pa ci lta ba bzhin du rgyal bar 'gyur ro//tshangs pa dang khyab dang/dbang phyug chen po dus kun du 'ong bar 'gyuro//gnod sbyin

232a5(rgyud): swA+e ta ya swA+e ta ya/hUM hUM hUM/badzra d+ha ra a gra/hUM hUM/bI da ra ya bI da ra ya/hUM tsan d+ha da an hA ka swa ta ya swAhA//dam tshig rtse mo'i gzi brjid kyi dam tshag ces bya ba'i rdo rje 'o//ang gi sngags 'di yino/
232a6(rgyud): na ma sa man ta badzra nAM/tri lo tsa na/i hi ba ra ba la/e gra;e hi sarba b+hud d+ha b+ho te swa ta naM/a ti sta naM/a ho sha sa ka sha sa ka/mahA bA ra badzra tra ma/ha sa ha/sa ma sa ma/d+ha ra d+ha ra/na ma stu te swAhA//
232a7(rgyud): rdo rje'i mtshan ma ni phyi rol gyi dkyil 'khor gyi byang phyogs kyi sgor bri'o//rdo rje klu'i sbrul gyi phyag rgya dang/'dul byed ki seng ge lta bu'i phyag rgya dag ni 'gram gnyis su dgod par bya'o//de dag

232b2(rgyud): brang 'gro'i phyag rgya'o//na ma sa man ta badzra NAM/a ti bi Sh+ya a ti bi Sh+ya/tri la tri la ba la ba la/ba ra ba ra/ta Ti ta Ti/tA TA tA TA/da ha da ha/b+hin d+ha b+hin d+ha/tsin ta tsin Ta/ban d+ha ban d+ha phaT//
